# TRMDIP 1 ~ 49 리워크 이전 외교 이벤트

country_event = {
	id = TRM_event.1
	title = TRM_event.1.t
	desc = TRM_event.1.desc
	picture = GFX_event_TRN_event_1
	
	fire_only_once = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = { 
		name = TRM_event.1.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = Trinity_Misono_Mika_Portrait_Rule
					option = Trinity_Mika_Portrait_ON_3
				}
			}
			set_portraits = {
				character = TRN_Misono_Mika
				civilian = { large = GFX_TRN_Misono_Mika_Portrait_Kon_ka }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = Trinity_Nagisa_Portrait_Rule
					option = Trinity_Nagisa_Portrait_ON
				}
			}
			set_portraits = {
				character = TRN_Kirifuji_Nagisa
				civilian = { large = GFX_TRN_Kirihuji_Nagisa_Portrait_Kon_ka }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = Trinity_Seia_Portrait_Rule
					option = Trinity_Seia_Portrait_ON
				}
			}
			set_portraits = {
				character = TRN_tpdldk
				civilian = { large = GFX_TRN_Yurizono_Seia_Portrait_Kon_ka }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = Trinity_Misono_Mika_Portrait_Rule
					option = Trinity_Mika_Portrait_Maki
				}
			}
			set_portraits = {
				character = TRN_Misono_Mika
				civilian={
					large= GFX_Maki_portrait
					small= GFX_Maki
				}
				army={
					large= GFX_Maki_portrait
					small= GFX_Maki
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #게헨나 항복 이벤트
	id = TRM_event.15
	title = TRM_event.15.t
	desc = TRM_event.15.desc
	picture = GFX_event_TRM_event_15

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = { set_global_flag = TRINITY_WON_THE_GEHENNA }
	trigger = {
		original_tag = TRM
		OR = {
			has_completed_focus = TRM_invasion_of_devils
			has_completed_focus = TRM_war_with_gehenna
		}
		has_government = fascism
		NOT = { has_global_flag = TRINITY_WON_THE_GEHENNA }
        TRM = {
            count_triggers = {
                amount = 3
                controls_state = 59
                controls_state = 56
                controls_state = 57
                controls_state = 55
                controls_state = 51
                controls_state = 1022
                controls_state = 1025
                controls_state = 42
                controls_state = 50
                controls_state = 54
                controls_state = 53
                controls_state = 52
                controls_state = 8
            }
        }
		GEM = {
			OR = {
				exists = no
				has_capitulated = yes
				is_subject = yes
			}
		}
		GEH = {
			OR = {
				exists = no
				has_capitulated = yes
				is_subject = yes
			}
		}
		HEU = {
			OR = {
				exists = no
				has_capitulated = yes
				is_subject = yes
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { 
		days = 5
	}
	option = {
		name = TRM_event.15.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_focus_tree = TRM_focus_tree }
			complete_national_focus = TRM_after_war
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = trinity.41 days = 03 }
		}
		set_country_flag = TRM_after_war_flag_decision_active
		unlock_decision_category_tooltip = TRN_after_war_decisions
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = TRM_mika_trinity
			add_idea = TRM_mika_trinity_2
		}
		swap_ruler_traits = {
			remove = TRN_mika_trait_1
			add = TRN_mika_trait_3
		}
        hidden_effect = { 
			news_event = { id = BA_news.5 } 
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # 헝가리 지지 선언
	id = TRMDIP.4
	title = TRMDIP.4.t
	desc = TRMDIP.4.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write

	is_triggered_only = yes

	
	option = { 
		name = BBA_italy_interaction_events.30.a 

		ai_chance = { 
			base = 100
		}

		ROOT = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
			    target = FROM
			    modifier = ITA_recognized_our_claims
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			give_guarantee = ROOT
		}
		ROOT = {
			give_guarantee = FROM
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					45 = {
						is_claimed_by = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			45 = {
				add_claim_by = ROOT
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				45 = {
					is_claimed_by = FROM
				}
			}
			45 = {
				remove_claim_by = FROM
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					45 = {
						is_controlled_by = FROM
					}
					any_country = {
						is_subject_of = FROM
						controls_state = 45
					}
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				transfer_state = 45
			}
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					is_subject_of = FROM
					controls_state = 45
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = { controls_state = 45 }
				country_event = {
					id = BBA_italy_interaction_events.17
					days = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# blasphemy that is
	option = { 
		name = TRMDIP.4.b
		ai_chance = { 
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

# 좋은 말로 할때 게헨나 영토를 반환하세요
country_event = { 
	id = TRN_after_war_event.40
	title = TRN_after_war_event.40.t
	desc = TRN_after_war_event.40.desc
	picture = GFX_event_none_picture
	
	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = { # 네 반환할게요.. ㅜㅜㅜ
		name = TRN_after_war_event.40.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				add = -1000
				GEM = {
					any_core_state = {
						is_owned_by = ROOT
						is_core_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			country_event = { id = TRN_after_war_event.41 days = 01 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			effect_tooltip = {
				FROM = { 
					add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = medium_increase } 
				}
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = GEM
							is_core_of = GEH
						}
						is_owned_by = ROOT
					}
				}
				transfer_state_to = FROM
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { # 제 땅인데? 꺼지셈 ㅋㅋ
		name = TRN_after_war_event.40.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				GEM = {
					any_core_state = {
						is_owned_by = ROOT
						is_core_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			country_event = { id = TRN_after_war_event.42 days = 01 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			effect_tooltip = {
				FROM = { 
					add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = medium_decrease } 
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = { is_faction_leader = yes }
						ROOT = { is_in_faction_with = FROM }
					}
					FROM = { remove_from_faction = ROOT }
				}
				create_wargoal = {
					target = ROOT
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}

		}

country_event = { 
	id = TRN_collapse_event.2
	title = TRN_collapse_event.2.t
	desc = TRN_collapse_event.2.desc
	picture = GFX_event_redemption

	fire_only_once = no 
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			TRM_collapse_event_appearance_effect = yes

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_none_flag } #더이상 봉기할 것이 없음.
				#무언가 작업을 해야 함
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_none_flag
			}

			event_target:TRM_collapse_event_target = {
				add_ideas = BA_GER_defense
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #
		name = TRN_collapse_event.2.a
		activate_mission = TRM_calm_storm
		
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = TRM
				NOT = {
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_week
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_fail
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.4 hours = 12 }
		}
		TRM_country_creation_over_effect = yes
	}
	option = { #
		name = TRN_collapse_event.2.b
		activate_mission = TRM_calm_storm
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
		
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = TRM
				NOT = {
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_week
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_fail
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.4 hours = 12 }
		}
		TRM_country_creation_over_effect = yes

		event_target:TRM_collapse_event_target  = {
			change_tag_from = ROOT
		}
	}
}
country_event = { 
	id = TRN_collapse_event.3
	title = TRN_collapse_event.3.t

	desc = {
		text = TRN_collapse_event.3.desc
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_none_flag }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = TRN_collapse_event.3.baddesc
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_none_flag
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_event_redemption

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		TRM_collapse_event_scripted_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			TRM_collapse_event_appearance_effect = yes

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_none_flag } #더이상 봉기할 것이 없음.
				news_event = { id = BA_news.17 hours = 8 }
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_political_power_cost = 0.10 }
			}
			
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRN_collapse_idea } } }
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRN_collapse_idea }
				set_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_political_power_cost = 0.05 }
				set_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_stability_factor = -0.05 }
				set_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_war_support_factor = -0.05 }
				
				set_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_attack_factor = -0.05 }
				set_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_defence_factor = -0.05 }
				set_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_morale_factor = -0.05 }
				set_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_org_factor = -0.05 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_political_power_cost = 0.10 }
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_stability_factor = -0.05 }
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_war_support_factor = -0.05 }
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_attack_factor = -0.10 }
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_defence_factor = -0.10 }
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_morale_factor = -0.05 }
				add_to_variable = { TRN_collapse_idea_army_org_factor = -0.05 }
			}

			event_target:TRM_collapse_event_target = {
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = BA_GER_defense } }
					add_ideas = BA_GER_defense_week
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #
		name = TRN_collapse_event.3.a
		add_political_power = -50
		add_war_support = -0.15
			
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = TRM
				NOT = {
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_week
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_fail
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.4 hours = 12 }
		}
		TRM_country_creation_over_effect = yes
	}
	option = { #
		name = TRN_collapse_event.2.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}

		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = TRM
				NOT = {
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_week
					has_idea = BA_GER_defense_fail
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.4 hours = 12 }
		}
		TRM_country_creation_over_effect = yes

		event_target:TRM_collapse_event_target  = {
			change_tag_from = ROOT
		}
	}
}

			clr_country_flag = KPD_start_KPD_flag

		}

			tooltip = KPD_country_collapse_value_tt
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = KPD_start_KPD_flag } }
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_KPD_side_KPD_var = 0 }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TRM_start_KPD_flag } }
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_KPD_side_TRM_var = 0 }
		}
	}

		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TRM_start_KPD_flag } }
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_KPD_side_TRM_var = 0 }
		}

		}
	}

			add_to_variable = {

			TRN_tpdldk = { clr_character_flag = TRN_fake_death_flag }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TRM_sanctus_loyalty_5_tt
			add_to_variable = { TRM_sanctus_loyalty = 5 }
			TRM_internal_factions_loyalty_change = yes

			custom_effect_tooltip = TRM_sanctus_popularity_15_tt
			add_to_variable = { TRM_sanctus_popularity = 15 }
			TRM_internal_factions_popularity_change = yes
			
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = communism
				long_name = TRM_seia_communism_party_long
				name = TRM_seia_communism_party
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = TRN_tpdldk
						ruling_only = no
					}
				}
				promote_character = TRN_tpdldk
			}
			else = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = TRN_tpdldk
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = marxism
						traits = {
							TRN_seia_trait
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_stability = -0.02

		add_trait = {
			character = TRN_tpdldk
			slot = political_advisor
			trait = b_dPdjsdmleocjstk
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_trait = {
				character = TRN_tpdldk
				slot = political_advisor
				trait = TRN_Hurt_Seia
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { 
	id = TRM_event.402
	title = TRM_event.402.t
	desc = TRM_event.402.desc
	picture = GFX_event_trm_event_sunjun
	fire_only_once = yes 
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = TRM_event.402.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				FROM = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
			}
			remove_from_faction = FROM
		} else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
			}
			leave_faction = yes
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			target = FROM
			type = annex_everything
		}
	}
}

	desc = TRN_faction_event.131.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_ruins

	trigger = {
		tag = TRM
		has_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = { 
		name = TRN_faction_event.131.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = strikes_1 }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = strikes_1
				add_idea = strikes_2
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_idea = strikes_2 }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = strikes_2
				add_idea = strikes_3
			}
		}
		else =  {
			add_ideas = strikes_1 
		}
		set_country_flag = crisis_in_progress

		custom_effect_tooltip = TRN_tt_popularity__20_tt
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_target = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_var = -20 }
		TRM_internal_factions_popularity_add = yes
		
		clr_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
	}

country_event = {
	id = TRN_faction_event.221
	title = TRN_faction_event.221.t
	desc = TRN_faction_event.221.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_factory

	trigger = {
		tag = TRM
		has_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = ROOT
					free_building_slots = {
						building = industrial_complex
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
						
				save_global_event_target_as = TRN_development_of_specific_state_event_target
	
				set_variable = { TRN_development_of_specific_state_factory_goal1 = industrial_complex_level }
				set_variable = { TRN_development_of_specific_state_factory_goal2 = arms_factory_level }
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = TRN_faction_event.221.a
		activate_mission = TRN_development_of_specific_state
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			event_target:TRN_development_of_specific_state_event_target = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = arms_factory
					level = 1
					instant_build = no
				}
			}
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = TRN_tt_popularity__10_tt
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_target = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_var = -10 }
		TRM_internal_factions_popularity_add = yes
		
		set_variable = { TRN_development_of_specific_state_faction_var = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
		clr_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
		set_global_flag = { #60일 동안 다른거 안시킴
			flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
			days = 60
			value = 1
		}
	}
}

	title = TRN_faction_event.231.t
	desc = TRN_faction_event.231.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_destroyers

	trigger = {
		tag = TRM
		has_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = ROOT
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good }
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_bad }
					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good days = 240 }
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = ROOT
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good }
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_bad }
					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good days = 240 }
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = ROOT
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good }
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_bad }
					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good days = 240 }
			}
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = ROOT
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good }
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_bad }
					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_bad days = 240 }
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = TRN_faction_event.231.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good }
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_good days = 240 }
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_bad }
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_government_plan_bad days = 240 }
			}
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = TRN_tt_popularity__20_tt
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_target = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_var = -20 }
		TRM_internal_factions_popularity_add = yes
		
		clr_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
	}

	id = TRN_faction_event.232
	title = TRN_faction_event.232.t
	desc = TRN_faction_event.232.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_spr_carlists_franco

	trigger = {
		tag = TRM
		has_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag

		OR = { 
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { TRM_one_loyalty < 30 }
				check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_one_var = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
				check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_one_var = -1 }
			}
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { TRM_two_loyalty < 30 }
				check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_two_var = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
				check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_two_var = -1 }
			}
			NOT = {
				check_variable = { TRM_three_loyalty < 30 }
				check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_three_var = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
				check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_three_var = -1 }
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { 
					modifier = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_one_var = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
							check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_one_var = -1 }
							check_variable = { TRM_one_loyalty < 30 }
						}
						factor = 0
					}
					set_variable = { TRN_faction_event_3_var = TRM_internal_factions_slot_one_var }
				}
				10 = { 
					modifier = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_two_var = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
							check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_two_var = -1 }
							check_variable = { TRM_two_loyalty < 30 }
						}
						factor = 0
					}
					set_variable = { TRN_faction_event_3_var = TRM_internal_factions_slot_two_var }
				}
				10 = { 
					modifier = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_three_var = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
							check_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_slot_three_var = -1 }
							check_variable = { TRM_three_loyalty < 30 }
						}
						factor = 0
					}
					set_variable = { TRN_faction_event_3_var = TRM_internal_factions_slot_three_var }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { 
		name = TRN_faction_event.232.a
		
		custom_effect_tooltip = TRN_tt_popularity__5_tt
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_target = TRN_faction_event_2_var }
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_var = -5 }
		TRM_internal_factions_popularity_add = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = TRN_tt3_popularity__20_tt
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_target = TRN_faction_event_3_var }
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_popularity_var = -20 }
		TRM_internal_factions_popularity_add = yes
		
		custom_effect_tooltip = TRM_tt3_loyalty__10_tt
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_loyalty_target = TRN_faction_event_3_var }
		set_variable = { TRM_internal_factions_loyalty_var = -10 }
		TRM_internal_factions_loyalty_add = yes
		
		clr_global_flag = TRN_faction_event_2_flag
	}

		}

country_event = { # 문제 - 노조 파업
	id = trnsocial.2
	title = trnsocial.2.t
	desc = trnsocial.2.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_deutsche_arbeitsfront

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no


	option = { # 탄압한다
		name = trnsocial.2.a
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
		add_manpower = -100
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = -100
		}
		add_days_mission_timeout = {
			mission = TRN_Mika_social_problem
			days = 40
		}
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = TRN_strike_season_ns
			days = 21
		}
		add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.025 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor > -0.50 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor = TRM_monthly_conscription_factor_increase }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = TRM.TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor
				min = -0.50
			}
			if = { #Apply penalties if CG is 100% or more
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_penalty_flag }
					check_variable = { var = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor value = -0.50 compare = less_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_country_flag = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_penalty_flag
				add_to_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_consumer_goods_expected_value = TRM_propaganda_expected_consumer_goods_penalty }
				subtract_from_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_industrial_capacity_factory = TRM_propaganda_factory_output_penalty }
				subtract_from_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_industrial_capacity_dockyard = TRM_propaganda_dockyard_output_penalty }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_penalty_flag
				check_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor > -0.50 }
			}
			clr_country_flag = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_penalty_flag
			subtract_from_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_consumer_goods_expected_value = TRM_propaganda_expected_consumer_goods_penalty }
			add_to_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_industrial_capacity_factory = TRM_propaganda_factory_output_penalty }
			add_to_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_industrial_capacity_dockyard = TRM_propaganda_dockyard_output_penalty }
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = TRN_Mika_social_problem_tt
	}
	option = { # 타협한다
		name = trnsocial.2.b
		ai_chance = { 
			base = 99
			modifier = {
				has_political_power > 99
				factor = 0.1
			}
		}

		add_political_power = -40
		add_popularity = { ideology = ROOT popularity = 0.015 }
	}
	option = { # 무시
		name = trnsocial.2.c
		ai_chance = { 
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				has_political_power < 99
				factor = 3
			}
		}
		
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = TRN_strike_season_ns
			days = 30
		}
		add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.02 }
	}
